(98)

where
q_{:\mu}\equiv q_{;\mu}-q_{,\mu}
. Note that,
\delta_{\alpha;\mu}^{a}
does not vanishes. By omitting
\bar{\omega}_{\mu ab}
and taking the linear part in
\mathchoice{\bar{q}^{{{a}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu}}}}{\bar{q}^{{{a}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu}}}}{\bar{q}^{{{a}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu}}}}{\bar{q}^{{{a}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu}}}}
, it is given by
-\kappa\delta_{u}^{\beta}\partial_{\mu}\mathchoice{\bar{q}^{{{u}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.80504pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.0036pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\alpha}}}}{\bar{q}^{{{u}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.80504pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.0036pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\alpha}}}}{\bar{q}^{{{u}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.80504pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.0036pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\alpha}}}}{\bar{q}^{{{u}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.80504pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.0036pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\alpha}}}}\delta_{\beta}^{a}=-\kappa\partial_{\mu}\mathchoice{\bar{q}^{{{a}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\al . Using the same conditions, we get that
q_{\alpha:\mu}^{a}=-\kappa\delta_{u}^{\lambda}\partial_{\mu}\mathchoice{\bar{q}^{{{u}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.80504pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.0036pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\alpha}}}}{\bar{q}^{{{u}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.80504pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.0036pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\alpha}}}}{\bar{q}^{{{u}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.80504pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.0036pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\alpha}}}}{\bar{q}^{{{u}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.80504pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.0036pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\alpha}}}}q_{\lambda}^{a}
.

The contributions in Eq. (
98
), in momentum space, reads

\begin{split}&-\frac{\kappa}{2\xi}\eta_{ab}\eta_{cd}\eta^{\nu\beta}\{[p_{3}^{\mu}\delta_{v}^{a}\eta_{\mu\theta}\delta_{\nu}^{\gamma}\delta_{m}^{b}+p_{3}^{\mu}\delta_{\mu}^{a}\delta_{u}^{\lambda}\eta_{\mu\theta}\delta_{v}^{u}\delta_{\lambda}^{\gamma}\delta_{m}^{b}+p_{3}^{\mu}\delta_{u}^{\lambda}\delta_{v}^{u}\eta_{\nu\theta}\delta_{m}^{a}\delta_{\lambda}^{\gamma}\delta_{\nu}^{b}+p_{3}^{\mu}\delta_{\mu}^{\gamma}\delta_{m}^{a}\delta_{v}^{b}\eta_{\nu\theta}\\
&\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad-p_{3\nu}\delta_{v}^{a}\eta_{\tau\theta}\delta_{m}^{b}\eta^{\tau\gamma}-p_{3\nu}\delta_{\tau}^{a}\delta^{\tau}_{u}\delta_{v}^{u}\eta_{\lambda\theta}\delta_{m}^{a}\eta^{\lambda\gamma}][p_{1}^{\alpha}\delta_{\alpha}^{c}\delta_{\beta}^{\rho}\delta_{l}^{d}+p_{1}^{\alpha}\delta_{\alpha}^{\rho}\delta_{l}^{c}\delta_{\beta}^{d}-p_{1\beta}\delta_{\pi}^{c}\delta_{l}^{d}\eta^{\pi\rho}]\\
&+[p_{2}^{\mu}\delta_{\mu}^{a}\delta_{\nu}^{\gamma}\delta_{m}^{b}+p_{2}^{\mu}\delta_{\mu}^{\gamma}\delta_{m}^{a}\delta_{\nu}^{b}-p_{2\nu}\delta_{\tau}^{a}\delta_{m}^{b}\eta^{\tau\gamma}][p_{3}^{\alpha}\delta_{v}^{c}\eta_{\alpha\theta}\delta_{\beta}^{\rho}\delta_{l}^{d}+p_{3}^{\alpha}\delta_{\alpha}^{c}\delta_{u}^{\lambda}\delta_{v}^{u}\eta_{\beta\theta}\delta_{l}^{d}\delta_{\lambda}^{\rho}+p_{3}^{\alpha}\delta_{u}^{\lambda}\delta_{v}^{u}\eta_{\alpha\theta}\delta_{\lambda}^{\rho}\delta_{l}^{c}\delta_{\beta}^{d}+p_{3}^{\alpha}\delta_{\alpha}^{\rho}\delta_{l}^{c}\delta_{v}^{d}\eta_{\beta\theta}\\
&\quad\quad-p_{3\beta}\delta_{v}^{c}\eta_{\pi\theta}\delta_{l}^{d}\eta^{\pi\rho}-p_{3\beta}\delta_{\pi}^{c}\delta_{u}^{\pi}\delta_{v}^{u}\eta_{\lambda\theta}\delta_{l}^{d}\eta^{\lambda\rho}]\}\\
&+(p_{1},l,\rho)\leftrightarrow(p_{2},m,\gamma).\end{split}

(99)

endequation

Thus, collecting all contributions, one can find that the vertex
qq\bar{q}
is given by

Vertex
Qq\bar{q}

), we can obtain the contribution to the vertex

(without

+\kappa(\delta^{a\mu}\bar{q}^{\nu\,b}+\delta^{b\nu}\bar{q}^{\nu\,a})(Q_{\mu ap}Q_{\nu\;b}^{\;p\;}-Q_{\nu ap}Q_{\mu\;b}^{\;p\;}).

in which linear terms in the quantum tetrad field

were omitted.

From Eq. (

-\kappa\eta_{\mu\nu}\eta_{uv}\delta_{\alpha}^{u}\bar{q}^{v\,\alpha}c^{\star\nu}\partial^{\pi}\partial_{\pi}c^{\mu}

(128)

Now, we have the terms coming from
\bar{g}^{\mu\lambda}

,{\mu}}^{\alpha}\right)\right]^{,\mu}-\kappa c^{\star\nu}\eta_{ab}[\mathchoice{\bar{q}^{{{a\mu}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.19997pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.19997pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.54257pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.95897pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\bar{q}^{{{a\mu}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.19997pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.19997pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.54257pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.95897pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\bar{q}^{{{a\mu}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.19997pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.19997pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.54257pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.95897pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\bar{q}^{{{a\mu}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.19997pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.19997pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.54257pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.95897pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}(-\delta{{}^{b}_{\alpha}}c^{\alpha}_{,{\mu}})]_{,\nu}.\end{split}

(133)

Collecting all contributions, in the momentum space, we have the vertices:

\vbox{\hbox{\includegraphics[scale={1}]{Vcc.eps}}}\quad\quad i[V_{3^{\star}3\bar{1}}]_{\rho,\sigma,v\theta}(p_{1},p_{2},p_{3})

(134)

and

\vbox{\hbox{\includegraphics[scale={1}]{VCC1.eps}}}\quad\quad i[V_{{4}^{\star}4\bar{1}}]_{ab,cd,v\theta}(p_{1},p_{2},p_{3}),

(135)

where

\begin{split}[V_{{3}^{\star}3\bar{1}}]^{\rho,\sigma,v\theta}(p_{1},p_{2},p_{3})={}&\kappa[-2{p}_{2}^{\theta}{p}_{2}^{v}\eta^{\rho\sigma}-{p}_{3}^{\theta}{p}_{2}^{v}\eta^{\rho\sigma}+{p}_{2}^{2}\eta^{\rho\sigma}\eta^{v\theta}+{p}_{1}^{\rho}{p}_{2}^{v}\eta^{\theta\sigma}+{p}_{2}^{\theta}{p}_{1}^{\rho}\eta^{v\sigma}-{p}_{2}^{\rho}{p}_{1}^{v}\eta^{\theta\sigma}\\
&-{p}_{1}^{\theta}{p}_{2}^{\rho}\eta^{v\sigma}-{p}_{3}^{\theta}{p}_{2}^{\rho}\eta^{v\sigma}+{p}_{3}^{\rho}{p}_{2}^{v}\eta^{\theta\sigma}+{p}_{2}^{\theta}{p}_{3}^{\rho}\eta^{v\sigma}-{p}_{3}^{\sigma}{p}_{1}^{v}\eta^{\theta\rho}-{p}_{3}^{\sigma}{p}_{2}^{v}\eta^{\theta\rho}\\
&-{p}_{1}^{\theta}{p}_{3}^{\sigma}\eta^{v\rho}+{p}_{1}^{\rho}{p}_{3}^{\sigma}\eta^{v\theta}-\left({p}_{1}\cdot{p}_{2}\right)\eta^{\theta\rho}\eta^{v\sigma}-\left({p}_{1}\cdot{p}_{2}\right)\eta^{\theta\sigma}\eta^{v\rho}-\left({p}_{2}\cdot{p}_{3}\right)\eta^{\theta\rho}\eta^{v\sigma}]\end{split}

(136)

and

\begin{split}[V_{4^{\star}4\bar{1}}]_{ab,cd,v\theta}(p_{1},p_{2},p_{3})={}&\kappa I_{abcd}\eta_{v\theta}p_{2}^{\pi}p_{2\pi}-\kappa I_{abcd}\eta_{ef}p_{2\nu}p_{2\mu}(\delta^{e\mu}\delta_{v}^{\nu}\delta_{\theta}^{f}+\delta^{\nu f}\delta_{v}^{\mu}\delta_{\theta}^{e})-\kappa I_{abcd}p_{3\theta}p_{2v}\end{split}

(137)

Appendix B
Transversality of the tetrad self-energy

One can express the tetrad self-energy in terms of the basis (
74
) as follows

\begin{split}\bar{\Pi}\mathchoice{{}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.51666pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.51666pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.1029pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.50208pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}_{{{a}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{b}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{{}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.51666pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.51666pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.1029pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.50208pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}_{{{a}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{b}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{{}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.51666pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.51666pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.1029pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.50208pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}_{{{a}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{b}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{{}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.51666pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.51666pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.1029pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.50208pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}_{{{a}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{b}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}(k)={}&c_{1}\delta_{a}^{\mu}\delta_{b}^{\nu}+c_{2}\delta_{a}^{\nu}\delta_{b}^{\mu}+c_{3}\eta^{\mu\nu}\eta_{ab}+c_{4}\eta_{ab}\frac{k^{\mu}k^{\nu}}{k^{2}}+c_{5}\eta^{\mu\nu}\frac{k_{a}k_{b}}{k^{2}}\\
&+c_{6}\left(\delta_{b}^{\nu}\frac{k_{a}k^{\mu}}{k^{2}}+\delta_{a}^{\mu}\frac{k_{b}k^{\nu}}{k^{2}}\right)+c_{7}\left(\delta_{b}^{\mu}\frac{k_{a}k^{\nu}}{k^{2}}+\delta_{a}^{\nu}\frac{k_{b}k^{\mu}}{k^{2}}\right)+c_{8}\frac{k_{a}k_{b}k^{\mu}k^{\nu}}{k^{4}},\end{split}

(138)

where
c_{i}
are scalar functions with mass dimension
2
.

The transversality condition (
61
) leads to the following constraints:

c_{1}+c_{6}=0,\quad c_{2}+c_{7}=0,\quad c_{3}+c_{4}=0\quad\text{and}\quad c_{5}+c_{6}+c_{7}+c_{8}=0.

(139)

Using these relation in Eq. (
138
), we obtain the transverse expression

\begin{split}&c_{1}(\xi,\zeta)\left(\delta_{a}^{\mu}-\frac{k_{a}k^{\mu}}{k^{2}}\right)\left(\delta_{b}^{\nu}-\frac{k_{b}k^{\nu}}{k^{2}}\right)+c_{2}(\xi,\zeta)\left(\delta_{a}^{\nu}-\frac{k_{a}k^{\nu}}{k^{2}}\right)\left(\delta_{b}^{\mu}-\frac{k_{b}k^{\mu}}{k^{2}}\right)\\
&\quad\quad+\left(c_{3}(\xi,\zeta)\eta_{ab}+c_{5}(\xi,\zeta)\frac{k_{a}k_{b}}{k^{2}}\right)\left(\eta^{\mu\nu}-\frac{k^{\mu}k^{\nu}}{k^{2}}\right),\end{split}

(140)

where
\xi
and
\zeta
are the gauge parameters introduced in Eq. (
12
).

is the

) and

.

(142)

where
n=1,2,\ldots,8
.

Now, we have to compute the scalar integrals
(T_{qq}^{(n)})\mathchoice{{}^{{{a}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{b}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.51666pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.51666pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.1029pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.50208pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}}{{}^{{{a}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{b}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.51666pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.51666pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.1029pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.50208pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}}{{}^{{{a}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{b}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.51666pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.51666pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.1029pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.50208pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}}{{}^{{{a}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{b}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.51666pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.51666pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.1029pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.50208pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}}[\bar{\Pi}^{(\text{I})}]\mathchoice{{}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.51666pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.51666pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.1029pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.50208pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}_{{{a}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{b}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{{}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.51666pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.51666pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.1029pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.50208pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}_{{{a}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{b}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{{}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.51666pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.51666pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.1029pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.50208pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}_{{{a}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{b}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{{}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.51666pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.51666pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.1029pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.50208pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}_{{{a}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{b}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}
, which have the following form

\int\frac{\mathop{d^{D}p}}{(2\pi)^{D}}s^{(\text{I})}(p,q,k),

(143)

where
p
is taken to be the loop momentum,
q=p+k
,
k
is the external momentum and
s^{(\text{I})}(p,q,k)
are scalar functions of the
p\cdot k
,
q\cdot k
,
p\cdot q
,
p^{2}
,
q^{2}
and
k^{2}
. We can simplify these scalar functions using the relations

\displaystyle p\cdot k={}

\displaystyle\frac{q^{2}-p^{2}-k^{2}}{2},

(144a)

\displaystyle q\cdot k={}

\displaystyle\frac{q^{2}+k^{2}-p^{2}}{2},

(144b)

\displaystyle p\cdot q={}

\displaystyle\frac{p^{2}+q^{2}-k^{2}}{2};

(144c)

which reduces
s^{(\text{I})}(p,q,k)
to combinations of powers of
p^{2}
and
q^{2}
.
In the end, the integrals in Eq. (
143
) becomes a combination of the simple integrals
[
34
]
:

I^{lm}\equiv\int\frac{\mathop{d^{D}p}}{(2\pi)^{D}}\frac{1}{(p^{2})^{l}(q^{2})^{m}}=i^{1+D}\frac{(k^{2})^{D/2-l-m}}{(4\pi)^{D/2}}\frac{\Gamma(l+m-D/2)}{\Gamma(l)\Gamma(m)}\frac{{\Gamma(D/2-m)\Gamma(D/2-l)}}{\Gamma(D-m-l)}.

(145)

The only non-vanishing integrals are

\displaystyle I_{11}={}

\displaystyle i^{1+D}\frac{(k^{2})^{D/2-2}}{(4\pi)^{D/2}}\frac{\Gamma(2-D/2)\Gamma(D/2-1)^{2}}{\Gamma(D-2)},

(146a)

\displaystyle I_{12}={}

\displaystyle I_{21}=\frac{3-D}{k^{2}}I_{11},

(146b)

\displaystyle I_{22}={}

\displaystyle\frac{(3-D)(6-D)}{k^{4}}I_{11}.

(146c)

Other integrals comes from massless tadpole-like contributions that go to zero when dimensional regularization is used
[
35
]
. Note that, in the massive case, these contributions may not vanish. The procedure described here for the massless case can be modified accordingly to the massive case, which could be required to the diagonal formulation of the EC theory in first-order form.

In
D=4-2\epsilon
dimensions, the ultraviolet pole
1/\epsilon
part of the integral
I^{11}
is given by

I_{\text{UV}}=\frac{i}{16\pi^{2}\epsilon}.

(147)

From Eq. (
146
), we see that if the UV pole of the integrals in Eq. (
143
) vanish, then the entire integrals vanish. This implies that, if the background tetrad self-energy
\bar{\Pi}_{\mu\nu}^{ab}
is finite, then it must vanish.

Summing all the contributions of the diagrams in Fig.
1
, we find that the divergent part of the background tetrad self-energy is given by (see Eq. (

))

\mathchoice{{\Pi}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.51666pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.51666pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.1029pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.50208pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}_{{{a}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{b}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{{\Pi}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.51666pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.51666pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.1029pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.50208pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}_{{{a}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{b}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{{\Pi}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.51666pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.51666pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.1029pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.50208pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}_{{{a}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox aystyle$}}{\makebox[3.51666pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.1029pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.50208pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}_{{{a}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{b}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{(T_{qq}^{(m)})^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.51666pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.51666pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.1029pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.50208pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}_{{{a}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{b}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{(T_{qq}^{(m)})^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.51666pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.51666pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.1029pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.50208pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}_{{{a}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{b}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}},

(148)

where

\begin{split}C_{1}={}&\left(\frac{\xi}{24}+\frac{961}{960}\right)k^{2}+\frac{\zeta}{2},\\
C_{2}={}&\left(\frac{\xi}{24}-\frac{123}{320}\right)k^{2}-\frac{\zeta}{4},\\
C_{3}={}&-\left(\frac{\xi}{6}+\frac{197}{480}\right)k^{2}-\frac{\zeta}{4},\\
C_{4}={}&-C_{3},\\
C_{5}={}&\left(\frac{7\xi}{12}+\frac{241}{240}\right)k^{2}+\frac{\zeta}{4},\\
C_{6}={}&-C_{1},\\
C_{7}={}&-C_{2},\\
C_{8}={}&-C_{5}+C_{1}+C_{2}.\end{split}

(149)

The above relations between the coefficients
C
are exactly the transversality conditions derived in appendix B (see Eq. (
139
)).

The gauge parameter
\zeta
does not appear in the coefficient
C_{8}
, since non-local terms, as

\zeta\frac{k_{a}k_{b}k_{\mu}k_{\nu}}{k^{2}}\xrightarrow[\text{space}]{\text{position}}\zeta\frac{\partial_{a}\partial_{b}\partial_{m}\partial_{\nu}}{\partial^{2}},

(150)

would arise. Such terms are forbidden due to the locality of gauge transformation and the corresponding BRST transformations.

Moreover, the following relation holds

\begin{split}P_{Qq}(p)\cdot V_{qq\bar{q}}(p,q,k)\cdot P_{qQ}(q)={}&0.\end{split}

(151)

This implies that the diagrams (e) must vanish at the integrand level, which is consistent with our computations.

(152)

From the EH action, we can obtain the quadratic form

B_{hh}=\int\mathop{d^{4}x}h_{\mu\nu}X^{\mu\nu\rho\sigma}h_{\rho\sigma}.

(153)

Using Eq. (
152
), we find that

(154)

Thus, we see that the quadratic terms in the tetrad would be equal to

B_{qq}=\delta_{\alpha}^{\mu}\eta_{\beta a}\delta_{\rho}^{\nu}\eta_{\sigma b}\left(X^{\alpha\beta\rho\sigma}+X^{\beta\alpha\rho\sigma}+X^{\alpha\beta\sigma\rho}+X^{\beta\alpha\sigma\rho}\right).

(155)

, we find that (

) is equal to (
155
). This allows us compare the propagators in the second order formalism.

). Using the propagator computed in Eq. (

), Eq. (

) leads (in the de Donder-like gauge

) to

-\frac{i}{p^{2}}\left[\frac{2\eta_{\sigma\rho}\eta_{\mu\nu}}{D-2}-\eta_{\sigma\mu}\eta_{\nu\rho}-\eta_{\sigma\nu}\eta_{\mu\rho}+(1+\xi)\frac{p_{\rho}\left(p_{\nu}\eta_{\sigma\mu}+p_{\mu}\eta_{\sigma\nu}\right)+p_{\sigma}\left(p_{\nu}\eta_{\mu\rho}+p_{\mu}\eta_{\nu\rho}\right)}{p^{2}}\right],

(159)

which is the graviton propagator in the de Donder gauge
[
15
]
.
